!Sample_title = 20-637-002_het_D0
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7663647
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 28 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Jul 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 28 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell line: BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: iPSC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: het
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_CT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D0
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. !Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.

Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38

!Sample_relation = SRA: https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sra?term=SRX21167004

^SAMPLE = GSM7663663
!Sample_title = 20-637-033_wt_D0
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7663663
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 28 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Jul 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 28 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell line: BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: iPSC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: wt
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_CC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D0
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_CC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D0
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell line: BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: iPSC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: hom
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_TT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D6
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.

The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_TT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.

!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.

^SAMPLE = GSM7663700
!Sample_title = 20-637-070_hom_D6
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7663700
!Sample_status = Public on Aug 28 2023
!Sample_submission_date = Jul 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 28 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell line: BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: iPSC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: hom
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_TT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D6
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_CT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D0
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell line: BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: iPSC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: hom
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_TT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D6
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: het
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_CT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D2
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_TT
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D23
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38

!Sample_submission_date = Jul 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Aug 28 2023
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell line: BIONi010-C-13
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: iPSC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = group: wt
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = genotype: rs148726219_CC
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = day: D23
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_growth_protocol_ch1 = The BIONi010-C-13 line (donor biosample SAMEA103988285) with doxycycline (DOX)-inducible neurogenin 2 (NGN2) transgene was obtained and is available from the European Bank for Induced pluripotent Stem Cells (EBiSC, www.ebisc.org). The line is derived from a dermal fibroblast sample donated by an 18 year-old healthy male of African-American descent. The EBiSC Bank acknowledges SAMEA103988285 as the source of human induced pluripotent stem cell line BIONi010-C-13 which was generated with support from EFPIA companies and the European Union (IMI-JU).
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = RNA was isolated from hiPSC-derived iNs at days 0, 2, 6, 13, and 23 using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit (Qiagen). Samples were extracted manually according to the manufacturer’s instructions or on a QIAcube using the AllPrep DNA/RNA mini kit protocol (Qiagen).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = For each timepoint, RNA from 4 technical replicates (4 independent wells of cells cultured simultaneously) were used per line. RNA library preparation from total RNA was conducted following the manufacturer’s protocol for the Kapa mRNA HyperPrep Kit (Roche). Briefly, 250 ng of total RNA was enriched for mRNA using magnetic oligo-dT beads. The remaining RNA was then fragmented by magnesium under elevated temperature. After fragmentation, RNA was depleted of rRNA and globin mRNA using the QIAseq FastSelect RNA Removal Kit (Qiagen). The depleted RNA then underwent first strand synthesis using reverse transcriptase and random primers. Combined second strand synthesis and A-tailing incorporated dUTP into the second cDNA strand for stranded RNA sequencing and added dAMP to the 3’ ends for adapter ligation. The cDNA fragments were then ligated to sequencing adaptors (IDT xGEN Dual Index UMI adapters) and was enriched using 16 cycles of PCR. Final libraries were assessed using the Agilent Tapestation and Qubit (ThermoFisher) assay methods then sequenced on an Illumina HiSeq 4000 sequencer using 2 x 75bp read length.
!Sample_data_processing = Raw fastq reads were subjected to quality control using FastQC (v0.11.7) and quality profiling, filtering for low quality and short reads, adapter trimming and mismatch correction using fastp (v0.20.1)
!Sample_data_processing = RNA reads were aligned to the hg38 build of the reference human genome, with transcript annotations from GENCODE, using the STAR aligner.
!Sample_data_processing = Gene expression was quantified using RSEM (RNA-Sequencing by Expectation Maximization)
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: FOSB_Raw_Counts_RSEM.txt is a tab-delimited text file containing raw counts for each sample (generated using RSEM)

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = CRISPR/Cas9-mediated SNP editing of BIONi010-C-13 was completed by the Genomic Engineering & iPSC Center (GEIC) at Washington University School of Medicine. To generate clones Heterozygous for rs148726219, the parental BIONi010-C-13 line was transfected with HiFi Cas9 v3 (IDT), gRNA with sequence GAGCTGTGGGTGGCTAGGGC, and ~60 bp single-stranded DNA oligos as homology arms flanking the PAM site (left HA: cccgggctctttctacccttaacactctggaaagcctgtgaaatgaaattattccacctcT; right HA: ctagccacccacagctctcctggtgctggtggcatcccccaaaacccactcccttcctac). An episomal vector expressing GFP was used for selection of transfected cells by FACS-based single-cell sorting. Clones were expanded and genotypes confirmed by next generation sequencing (NGS). The wild-type clone was generated in the same CRISPR experiment but was untargeted. To generate homozygous clones, heterozygous clones were re-targeted a second time. Directed differentiation of hiPSCs to excitatory iNs was performed as previously described, with modifications.
